	country = SPR

	style = 2
  	picture = "news_paper"

	trigger = {
		government = democratic
	}

	date = { day = 16 month = february year = 1936 }

	action_a = {
		ai_chance = 100
		name = EVT_2_ACTA
		command = { type = dissent value = 4 }
		command = { type = set_domestic which = political_left value = 7 }
		command = { type = headofstate which = 255011 }###
		command = { type = headofgovernment which = 255034 }
		command = { type = foreignminister which = 255062 }
		command = { type = armamentminister which = 255103 }
		command = { type = ministerofsecurity which = 255146 }
		command = { type = ministerofintelligence which = 255174 }
		command = { type = chiefofstaff which = 255204 }
		command = { type = chiefofarmy which = 255231 }
		command = { type = chiefofnavy which = 255252 }
		command = { type = chiefofair which = 255269 }
	}
	action_b = {
		ai_chance = 0
		name = EVT_2_ACTB
		command = { type = dissent value = 4 }
		command = { type = set_domestic which = political_left value = 4 }
	}
}

}

#ver 1

#event = { 
#	id = 5130
#	random = no
#	tag = { SPR EUS U52 }
#	persistent = yes
#	
#	name = "Spanish economic crisis - 1937"
#	desc = "While both sides of the conflict in Spain experienced some level of economic hardship, nothing compared to the utter financial chaos that gripped the Republican-held areas starting in 1937. On the surface, nothing seemed to indicate the coming economic catastrophe. Republicans started the war in control of many major developed industrial centers, sizable gold reserves of the Spanish central bank, the privelege of being recognized as a legitimate government of Spain, foreign backing of the Soviet Union and sympathizers in many other countries. The reasons behind the crisis are still not completely understood, and much blame is laid at the feet of the Madrid government, the revolutionary militias, and Moscow. While the Francoist side developed a pragmatic approach to the economy which proved relatively successful, the Republican side faced unyielding pressure from within to transform the Spanish economy in the midst of a war, and it ended up with massive distortions and inability to collect taxes. The process of collectivizations and nationalizations, aimed at developing a socialist economy for the post-war period, has been pointed to as the cause for this situation as most of the taxable economic agents were eradicated. Banks stopped paying interest and were expropriated, rents were not collected anymore, collectivized factories distributed their earnings among their workers and were therefore permanently decapitalized, and public limited companies stopped paying dividends. Republican collectivizations also had a lasting effect on the tourism sector, as the socialization of hotels, cafes, restaurants and spectacle venues led to a complete paralysis of the activity. Meanwhile, despite the Republic's status as an internationally recognized government, most foreign businesses began to avoid the Republican areas, afraid of having their assets arbitrarily nationalized or simply ransacked and stolen. Where possible, they redirected their trade to the Nationalist zone instead. In the end, the only way Madrid could continue to fund the war effort was through massive monetary emission, which resulted in equally massive hyperinflation, reaching 200 to 300 percent. This, in turn, led to the improverishment of the Spanish populace, disillusionment and demoralization, blame trading, infighting, and ultimately, to defeat. By 1938, the Republican Treasury had lost all its collection power and the country was left in a year of absolute fiscal anarchy, with rampant inflation and deficit. Civil servants gradually started going away and the Treasury directive board stopped holding its meetings."

event = { 
	id = 5130
	random = no
	tag = { SPR EUS U52 }
	persistent = yes
	
	name = EVT_5130_NAME
	desc = EVT_5130_DESC
	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = EVT_5130_ACTA
		trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = U52 } } }
		ai_chance = 100
		command = { type = dissent 		value = -2   }
		command = { type = money 		value = -200 }
		command = { type = free_money 	value = -3   }
		#command = { type = industrial_modifier which = total value = -8 }
		
		command = { type = event which = 5131 where = -1 when = 360 }
	}
	action = {
		name = EVT_5130_ACTB
		trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = U52 } } NOT = { policy = { type = political_left value = 10 } } }
		ai_chance = 0
		command = { type = dissent value = 15 }
		
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		
		command = { type = local_setflag when = 1 which = SPRRecovery value = 2 }
		command = { type = event which = 5131 where = -1 when = 360 }
	}
}

event = { 
	id = 5140
	random = no
	country = SPR

	name = EVT_5140_NAME
	desc = EVT_5140_DESC 
	style = 2
	
	trigger = {
		alliance = { country = U52 }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				event = { id = 5100 days = 290 } #May 1937
				control = { province = 355 data = -3 } #Land connection
			}
			lost_national 	= { country = U52 value = 30 }
			lost_VP 		= { country = U52 value = 30 }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 1 month = june year = 1936 }
	offset = 5
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1940 }
	
	action = {
		name = EVT_5140_ACTA
		ai_chance = 100
		command = { type = dissent value = 5 }
		command = { type = event which = 5141 where = U52 when = 0 }
	}
	action = {
		name = EVT_5140_ACTB
		ai_chance = 0
		command = { type = dissent value = 10 }
		command = { type = end_mastery which = U52 }
		command = { type = event which = 2400530 where = U52 when = 0 } #independent state
	}
}

		}

#6,791 trucks - 1 armored car brigade

#600 aircraft - 1 int, 1 tac, 1 cas
#1,000 artillery - 7 art brigades?
#122 tanks - 1 medium tank brigade
#500 trucks/40 armored cars - 1 armored car brigade

#Soviet:
#60,000 international brigades+2000 soviet advisers, 6 full strength divisions

event = { 
	id = 5200
	random = no
	country = SOV
	persistent = yes

	decision = {
		NOT = { event = 5203 } #end of intervention
		NOT = { alliance = { country = SPR } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = SPA } }
		war = { country = SPR country = SPA }
		NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_5 }
		NOT = { flag = SPR_NonCommie } #Anti-communist coup happened in spain
	}

	decision_trigger = { 
		NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_delay }
	}
	
	trigger = {
		NOT = { event = 5203 } #end of intervention
		NOT = { alliance = { country = SPR } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = SPA } }
		war = { country = SPR country = SPA }
		NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_5 }
		NOT = { flag = SPR_NonCommie } #Anti-communist coup happened in spain
		NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_delay }
		
		NOT = { #Soviets pulled support from spain shortly after the munich treaty, seeing the republican cause as doomed and not worth the price
			AND = {
				event = { id = 2003011 days = 90 } #Munich treaty
				region = { country = SPA region = "Spain" data = 1 value = 45 }
				OR = {
					region = { country = SPA region = "Spain" data = 1 value = 55 }
					NOT = { control = { province = 341 data = SPR } } #Madrid
					NOT = { control = { province = 357 data = SPR } } #Barcelona
				}
			}
		}
		
		#ai will do if:
		NOT = { war = { country = GER } }
		NOT = { war = { country = ENG } }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				ic = 50
				NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_1 }
				flag = { which = AidSPA when = 1 value = 1 } #Someone else has to intervene first
			}
			AND = {
				ic = 100
				NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_2 }
				flag = { which = AidSPA when = 1 value = 2 }
			}
			AND = {
				ic = 150
				NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_3 }
				flag = { which = AidSPA when = 1 value = 4 } #normal limit
			}
			AND = {
				ic = 200
				NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_4 }
				flag = { which = AidSPA when = 1 value = 6 }
			}
			AND = {
				ic = 250
				NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_5 }
				flag = { which = AidSPA when = 1 value = 8 }
			}
		}
	}
	
	name = EVT_5200_NAME
	desc = EVT_5200_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "SCW_Soviet_Intervention"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 5
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1999 }
	
	action = {
		name = EVT_5200_ACTA
		trigger = { NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_1 } }
		command = {	type = setflag which = AidSPR when = 1 value = 1 }
		command = { type = free_ic value = -1 }
		#equivalent of 2 IC daily
		command = { type = trade which = SOV where = SPR supplies = -10 money = 0.5 value = 1 when = 1800 }

		#Establishing low-strength units. The pace of replenishment will be controlled by the ability of Spain to supply these units, which is in turn assisted by the rate of supply transfer

		#International brigade 1
		command = { type = money value = -20 }
		#International brigade 2
		command = { type = money value = -20 }
		#International brigade 3
		command = { type = money value = -20 }
		#International brigade 4
		command = { type = money value = -20 }
		#Light tank brigade 1
		command = { type = money value = -50 }
		#Light tank brigade 2
		command = { type = money value = -50 }
		#Light tank brigade 2
		command = { type = money value = -50 }
		#art brigade 1
		command = { type = money value = -60 }
		#art brigade 2
		command = { type = money value = -60 }
		#art brigade 5 (full cost)
		command = { type = money value = -133 }
		
		command = { trigger = { ai = yes } type = money	value = 10 }
		
		command = { type = manpowerpool value = -30 }
	
		command = { type = remove_division which = 10848 value = 7036} #5.IABR
		command = { type = remove_division which = 10848 value = 7014} #3.IABR
		command = { type = remove_division which = 10848 value = 7018} #402.IABR
		command = { type = remove_division which = 10848 value = 7007} #2.TBABR
		command = { type = remove_division which = 10848 value = 7018} #32.LSHABR
		
		command = { type = belligerence value = 1.0 }
		command = { type = setflag which = SOV_to_SPR_1 }
		command = { type = event which = 5201 where = SPR when = 30 }
		command = { type = access which = SPR }
		command = { type = setflag which = SOV_to_SPR_delay }

		command = { type = local_setflag which = AID_to_SPR }
		command = { type = setflag which = SPR_SOVAssistFlag }
	}
	action = {
		name = EVT_5200_ACTB
		trigger = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_1 }
		command = {	type = setflag which = AidSPR when = 1 value = 1 }

		command = { trigger = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_2 NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_3 } } type = money value = -20 }

		command = { trigger = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_2 NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_3 } } type = money value = -20 }

		command = { trigger = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_2 NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_3 } } type = money value = -60 }

event = { 
	id = 5203
	random = no
	country = SOV
	#persistent = yes

	decision = { 
		flag = SOV_to_SPR_1
	}

	decision_trigger = {
		NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_delay }
	}
	
	trigger = {
		flag = SOV_to_SPR_1
		NOT = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_delay }
		OR = {
			NOT = { war = { country = SPR country = SPA } }
			war = { country = GER }
			flag = SPR_NonCommie #Anti-communist coup happened in spain
			AND = { #Soviets pulled support from spain shortly after the munich treaty, seeing the republican cause as doomed and not worth the price
				event = { id = 2003011 days = 90 } #Munich treaty
				region = { country = SPA region = "Spain" data = 1 value = 45 }
				OR = {
					region = { country = SPA region = "Spain" data = 1 value = 55 }
					NOT = { control = { province = 341 data = SPR } } #Madrid
					NOT = { control = { province = 357 data = SPR } } #Barcelona
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	name = EVT_5203_NAME
	desc = EVT_5203_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "SCW_Soviet_Intervention"
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 5
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1999 }
	
	action = {
		name = EVT_5203_ACTA
		command = { trigger = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_5 } type = free_ic value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_4 } type = free_ic value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_3 } type = free_ic value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_2 } type = free_ic value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { flag = SOV_to_SPR_1 } type = free_ic value = 1 }

		command = { type = belligerence value = -1.0 }
		command = { type = end_access which = SPR }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { alliance = { country = SPR } } } type = military_control which = SOV where = SPR value = 0 }
		command = { type = end_trades which = SPR where = SOV }
		
		command = { trigger = { exists = SPA } 				type = dissent value = 2  } #Defeated or withdrawn prematurely
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { exists = SPA } } 	type = dissent value = -2 } #Victory

		#Thus no matter how the civil war ends, the USSR is unlikely to recover much of the equipment that it sent over. Since 80% of the gold reserves were moved to USSR, it retains whatever gold spain still had left.
		#The Spanish gold ($518 million USD in 1936 dollars). (~$6.43 million cash was spent on the initial deliveries + the cost of 5 air units is approximately $250 million)

		command = { type = money  		value = 51800 }

event = {
	id = 5270
	random = no
	country = U56

	decision = {
		exists = SPA
		exists = SPR
		NOT = {	war = { country = SPA } }
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		event = { id = 5100 days = 400 } #Late 1937
		flag = ITA_to_SPA_2
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = SPA
		exists = SPR
		NOT = {	war = { country = SPA } }
		flag = ITA_to_SPA_2
		event = { id = 5100 days = 400 } #Late 1937
	}


	name = EVT_5270_NAME
	desc = EVT_5270_DESC 
	style = 2

	date = { day = 1 month = june year = 1936 }
	offset = 35
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1947 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_5270_ACTA
		ai_chance = 100
		command = { type = belligerence value = 1 }
		command = { type = access which = SPA }
        command = { type = relation which = SPA value = 20 }
		command = { type = dissent value = 2 }
		command = { type = money value = -100 } #most of the money is from private donations, not from the church itself
		command = { trigger = { ai = yes } type = money	value = 10 }
		
		command = { type = trigger which = 5271 }
	}
}

	}

	desc = EVT_5280_DESC 
	style = 2

	offset = 35
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1947 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_5280_ACTA
		ai_chance = 100
        command = { type = relation which = SPA value = 5 }
		command = { type = dissent value = -1 }
		command = { type = money value =  200 }
		command = { type = trade which = USA where = SPA supplies = -10 oil = -5 rare_materials = -1 money = 0.5 value = 1 when = 1800 }
		#SPA Per year
		#+3600 supplies
		#+1800 oil
		#+360  rare materials
		#-180  money
		
		command = { type = trigger which = 5281 }
	}
	action = {
		name = EVT_5280_ACTB
		ai_chance = 0
        command = { type = relation which = SPA value = -5 }
		command = { type = dissent value = 5 } #Against free market, business is not happy
		command = { type = money value = -100 }
	}
}

##############################################
# Spain - Experience gained

event = {
	id = 5290
	random = no
	tag = { SOV VIC GER ITA ENG }
	persistent = yes

	name = EVT_5290_NAME
	desc = EVT_2662_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "scw"
	
	action = {
		name = EVT_5290_ACTA
		trigger = {
			NOT = { AND = { local_flag = SCWExp war = { country = SPA country = SPR } } }
		}
		command = { type = dissent which = -0.2 }
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = SCWExp }
	}
	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_GREAT" # Great! 
		trigger = {
			local_flag = SCWExp
			war = { country = SPA country = SPR }
		}
		
		command = { type = gain_tech which = -1 }
		command = { type = local_setflag when = 1 which = SCWExp_count value = 1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = AID_to_SPR NOT = { government = communist } } type = dissent value = 2 } #+2 dissent if aiding the SPR but not a communist country
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = AID_to_SPA NOT = { government = fascist   } } type = dissent value = 2 } #+2 dissent if aiding the SPA but not a fascist country
		
		#Proxy wars get increasingly unpopular the longer they go on. Maximum +2 dissent every 6 months (after 10 years of war)
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 1  } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 2  } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 3  } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 4  } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 5  } } type = dissent value = 0.1 } #2.5 years - historical SCW end - 0.5 dissent
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 6  } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 7  } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 8  } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 9  } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 10 } } type = dissent value = 0.1 } #5 years
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 11 } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 12 } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 13 } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 14 } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 15 } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 16 } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 17 } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 18 } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 19 } } type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = { which = SCWExp_count when = 1 value = 20 } } type = dissent value = 0.1 } #10 years
		

		command = { type = event which = 5290 where = -1 when = 180 } #6 months
	}
}

event = { 
	id = 5300
	random = no
	country = SPR

	trigger = {
		war = { country = SPA }
		OR = {
			event = { id = 5100 days = 1000 } #around three years into the war
			event = { id = 2003011 days = 30 } #Munich treaty
			region = { country = -2 region = "Spain" data = 1 value = 80 }
		}
		OR = {
			AND = { #defeatist coup
				NOT = { alliance = { country = ENG } }
				OR = {
					lost_VP = { value = 35 } 
					region = { country = -2 region = "Spain" data = 1 value = 75 }
					AND = {
						region = { country = -2 region = "Spain" data = 1 value = 60 }
						OR = {
							NOT = { control = { province = 341 data = -3 } } #Madrid
							NOT = { control = { province = 357 data = -3 } } #Barcelona
						}
					}
					AND = {
						region = { country = -2 region = "Spain" data = 1 value = 50 }
						NOT = { control = { province = 341 data = -3 } } #Madrid
						NOT = { control = { province = 357 data = -3 } } #Barcelona
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				alliance = { country = ENG }
				region = { country = -3 region = "Spain" data = 1 value = 70 }
			}
		}
		NOT = { 
			alliance = { country = SOV }
		}
	} 

	name = EVT_5300_NAME
	desc = EVT_5300_DESC
	style = 2

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 35
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1999 } 

	action = { 
		name = EVT_5300_ACTA 
		command = { type = regime_falls }

		command = { type = headofgovernment 		which = 255274 } #Segismundo Casado Lopez

		#command = { type = ministerofintelligence 	which =  } 
		command = { type = chiefofstaff 			which = 255273 } #Segismundo Casado Lopez
		#command = { type = chiefofarmy 			which =  } 
		#command = { type = chiefofnavy 			which =  } 
		#command = { type = chiefofair 				which =  } 
		
		command = { type = switch_allegiance which = -1 where = REB }
		
		command = { type = remove_division which = "1st PCE-JSU Mil. Div." value = SPR }
		command = { type = remove_division which = "2nd PCE-JSU Mil. Div." value = SPR }
		command = { type = remove_division which = "3rd PCE-JSU Mil. Div." value = SPR }
		command = { type = remove_division which = "4th PCE-JSU Mil. Div." value = SPR }
		command = { type = remove_division which = "5th PCE-JSU Mil. Div." value = SPR }
		command = { type = remove_division which = "6th PCE-JSU Mil. Div." value = SPR }
		command = { type = remove_division which = "7th PCE-JSU Mil. Div." value = SPR }
		
		command = { type = remove_division which = "3rd PCE-JSU (PSUC) Mil. Div." value = SPR }
		
		#command = { type = switch_allegiance which = -1 where = REB }

event = { 
	id = 5322
	random = no
	persistent = yes

	name = EVT_5322_NAME
	desc = EVT_5322_DESC
	style = 2

	action = { 
		name = "ACTION_NAME_DAMN"
		command = { type = dissent value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = SPA } } 	type = secederegion 	which = SPA value = "Spain" when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = SPR } } 	type = secederegion 	which = SPR value = "Spain" when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { country = ENG } 			type = secedeprovince 	which = ENG value = 348 	when = 2 }
	} 
}


##############################################
# Spain - Ahistorical entry into the war
##############################################

event = { 
	id = 5350
	random = no
	country = SPA

	name = ACTION_NAME_JOIN_AXIS
	desc = EVT_5350_DESC
	style = 2
	
	decision = {
		war = { country = GER country = ENG }
		NOT = {	
			alliance = { country = GER }
			war = { country = GER }
			war = { country = SPR }
		}
		NOT = {
			flag = SPA_ENGAssistFlag
			flag = SPA_FRAAssistFlag
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			flag = SPA_GERAssistFlag
			flag = SPA_ITAAssistFlag
		}
		NOT = { 
			event = 2003200
			lost_national = { country = GER value = 20 }
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		war = { country = GER country = ENG }
		NOT = {	
			alliance = { country = GER }
			war = { country = GER }
			war = { country = SPR }
		}
		NOT = {
			flag = SPA_ENGAssistFlag
			flag = SPA_FRAAssistFlag
		}
		OR = {
			flag = SPA_GERAssistFlag
			flag = SPA_ITAAssistFlag
		}
		OR = {
			flag = SPR_ENGAssistFlag
			flag = SPR_FRAAssistFlag
			alliance = { country = POR country = GER } #Portugal joined the war
			#small chance Spain will join if Italy already joined and the border with France is undefended
			AND = { 
				war = { country = VIC country = ITA }
				NOT = { event = 2002008 }
				NOT = { year = 1942 }
				random = 10
				NOT = { garrison = { country = VIC province = 358 type = land size = 1 area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = VIC province = 326 type = land size = 1 area = 0 } }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			region = { region = "Benelux" country = GER data = 1 value = 30 } #Germany invading Belgium
			region = { region = "France"  country = GER data = 1 value = 5  } #Germany invading France 
		}
		NOT = { 
			event = 2003200
			lost_national = { country = GER value = 20 }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 20
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1999 } 

	action = { 
		name = EVT_5350_ACTA
		command = { type = alliance which = GER }
		command = { type = dissent value = 10 }
	}
}

event = { 
	id = 5351
	random = no
	country = SPR

	name = ACTION_NAME_JOIN_ALLIES
	desc = EVT_5351_DESC
	style = 2
	
	decision = {
		war = { country = GER country = ENG }
		NOT = {	
			alliance = { country = ENG }
			alliance = { country = SOV }
			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = SPA }
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				flag = SPR_ENGAssistFlag
				flag = SPR_FRAAssistFlag
			}
			flag = SPR_NonCommie
		}
		NOT = {
			flag = SPA_ENGAssistFlag
			flag = SPA_FRAAssistFlag
		}
		NOT = { 
			lost_national = { country = ENG value = 20 }
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		war = { country = GER country = ENG }
		NOT = {	
			alliance = { country = ENG }
			alliance = { country = SOV }
			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = SPA }
		}
		NOT = {
			flag = SPA_ENGAssistFlag
			flag = SPA_FRAAssistFlag
		}
		OR = {
			flag = SPA_GERAssistFlag
			flag = SPA_ITAAssistFlag
		}
		NOT = { 
			lost_national = { country = ENG value = 20 }
		}
		#If received assitance from England&France, or if anti-Commie coup happened, will join the Allies immediately
		#Otherwise, will wait for a good moment to join the Allies, such as if it looks like the Axis are losing
		OR = { 
			AND = {
				flag = SPR_ENGAssistFlag
				flag = SPR_FRAAssistFlag
			}
			flag = SPR_NonCommie
			AND = {
				war = { country = GER country = SOV }
				war = { country = GER country = USA }
				NOT = { flag = GER_USA_undeclared_war }
				OR = {
					lost_national = { country = GER value = 10 } #Germany invaded
					NOT = { region = { region = "Poland" country = GER data = 1 value = 30 } }
					alliance = { country = ENG country = POR } #portugal joined the allies
					event = 980 #Successful Allied landings in North Africa / negotiations with Darlan
					region = { region = "Morocco" country = USA data = 1 value = 60 }
					region = { region = "Morocco" country = ENG data = 1 value = 60 }
					event = 2003181 #July plot
				}
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 20
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1999 } 

	action = { 
		name = EVT_5351_ACTA
		command = { type = alliance which = ENG }
		command = { type = dissent value = 10 }
	}
}

		war = { country = GER country = SOV }

			alliance = { country = SOV }
			alliance = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = SOV }
			war = { country = SPA }
		}
		flag = SPR_SOVAssistFlag
		NOT = { flag = SPR_NonCommie }

			flag = SPA_GERAssistFlag
			flag = SPA_ITAAssistFlag
		}
		NOT = { 
			lost_national = { country = SOV value = 20 }
		}
		OR = {
			lost_national = { country = GER value = 30 } #Germany losing
			AND = {
				event = 2003181 #July plot
				war = { country = GER country = ENG }
				war = { country = GER country = USA }
				NOT = { region = { region = "Poland" country = GER data = 1 value = 30 } }
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 20

		command = { type = dissent value = 10 }

#########################
# Allied direct intervention if the SCW is still going on when war breaks out
#########################

event = { 
	id = 5360
	random = no
	country = ENG

	name = EVT_5360_NAME
	desc = EVT_5360_DESC
	style = 2
	
	decision = {
		war = { country = GER }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = SPR } }
		war = { country = SPR country = SPA }
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		flag = SPA_GERAssistFlag
	}
	trigger = {
		war = { country = GER }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = SPR } }
		war = { country = SPR country = SPA }
		flag = SPA_GERAssistFlag
		#Germany is busy in Poland or elsewhere
		NOT = {
			region = { region = "Benelux" country = GER data = 1 value = 30 }
			region = { region = "France"  country = GER data = 1 value = 5  }
		}
		control = { province = 341 data = SPR } # Madrid
	}

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 20
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1999 } 

	action = { 
		name = EVT_5360_ACTA
		command = { type = war which = SPA }
		command = { type = alliance which = SPR }
